# Agents · P · part 25

972 entries, pr-devops to pr-reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pr-devops**: Review affected build, deploy, infrastructure, permissions, secrets, observability, rollout, and recovery paths read-only. Report operational evidence and gaps without triggering jobs or applying changes. ([gear2000/llm-config-setup](https://github.com/gear2000/llm-config-setup/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-devops.md) · ★1)
- **pr-diagnose**: Reads a factory PR (diff, review notes, metadata) plus the issue's ACs and the project contracts, and returns a structured evidence digest — per concern: claim, location, what the code does, evidence, candidate fix. Locates and summarizes; does NOT render a merge verdict (the caller judges). Runs on Sonnet. ([saaga0h/themis](https://github.com/saaga0h/themis/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-diagnose.md))
- **pr-diff**: You receive a GitHub pull_request webhook payload. Extract PR_NUMBER and REPO, call get_pr_diff, then write: ([TGPSKI/leather](https://github.com/TGPSKI/leather/blob/HEAD/examples/11-high-volume-ci/agents/pr-diff.agent.md) · ★17 · 2 copies)
- **pr-diff**: Produces a clean diff of only what a PR or branch adds, in isolated context, without merge-commit noise. Resolves the merge-base, fetches PR metadata, excludes lockfiles and generated code, and returns a summary plus the diff. Use proactively at the start of any review workflow that needs the diff. Takes a PR number, branch, or range. Read-only. ([austintheriot/dotfiles](https://github.com/austintheriot/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-diff.md) · ★1)
- **pr-diff**: PR의 diff 전문을 읽고 확인 가능한 사실만 골라 돌려주는 감사자. 삭제된 파일, 총괄 문서 접촉, 시크릿, 테스트 없는 구현을 잡는다. 고치지 않고 취향으로 지적하지도 않는다. ([tkyoun0421/la-bie-belle](https://github.com/tkyoun0421/la-bie-belle/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-diff.md))
- **pr-diff-fetcher**: Fetches pull request or merge request data including diff, metadata, and commits ([storefront-x/storefront-x](https://github.com/storefront-x/storefront-x/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pull_request_diff_fetcher.md) · ★39)
- **pr-diff-reader**: Reads only the diff and reconstructs what the PR actually does, with no access to the author's explanation - an artificial reader that cannot be led by the write-up. Called from stage 1 (bidirectional inventory) of the plumb:pr-review skill. ([Haruki1090/plumb](https://github.com/Haruki1090/plumb/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-diff-reader.md))
- **pr-diff-reviewer**: Pre-PR diff review (multi-agent ensemble). Orchestrates 6 parallel reviewers (rules, bugs, git history, past-PR comments, inline comments, docs/rules consistency) + parallel confidence scoring, on a selectable model (Opus at max effort by default; Fable on demand). Run before gh pr create. Reports blockers/warnings/nits/low-confidence, surfaces out-of-scope defects as GitHub-issue drafts, instructs the caller to confirm fixes with the user via AskUserQuestion, and creates a marker file on success. ([radionest/clarinet](https://github.com/radionest/clarinet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-diff-reviewer.md))
- **pr-diff-scope**: Step 2 of run-on-pr. Computes one PR's ground truth: changed source files, sub-folders, and BASE-SIDE changed line ranges from a checkout pinned to the merge-base; ALSO extracts the PR's cited paper URLs (the paper-citation ground truth). Invoked by the run-on-pr skill. ([AI-native-Systems-Research/spotlights](https://github.com/AI-native-Systems-Research/spotlights/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-diff-scope.md) · ★6)
- **pr-digest**: List the open pull requests in every repo under /workspace using the GitHub API and `GITHUB_TOKEN`. For each one, give its title, author, age in days and a one-line summary of what it changes. Flag any pull request with no activity for more than three days. Reply with the report only. ([mnzsss/ivar](https://github.com/mnzsss/ivar/blob/HEAD/examples/agents/pr-digest.md) · ★5)
- **pr-director**: あなたはアトミックソフトウェア株式会社のPR業務ディレクターです。 「PRよろ」で呼び出され、依頼内容を分析して適切な専門エージェントに仕事を振ります。 自分では原稿を書かず、判断と委任に徹します。 ([Takanechis/cowork-agents](https://github.com/Takanechis/cowork-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-director.md))
- **pr-discover**: PR discovery and pre-analysis agent. Gathers all PR review threads, Codacy findings, runs code review subagents, and returns a structured findings report with file-based thread clusters. Dispatched by /pr-resolve to isolate the heavy data-gathering phases from main context. ([lbruton/StakTrakr](https://github.com/lbruton/StakTrakr/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-discover.md) · ★11 · 5 copies)
- **pr-discovery**: Product discovery agent that analyzes a project snapshot to identify what the product is, who it's for, and what problem it solves. Produces user personas, value propositions, and stage assessment. ([tsaol/awesome-claude](https://github.com/tsaol/awesome-claude/blob/HEAD/skills/product-review/agents/pr-discovery.md) · ★69)
- **pr-discovery**: Analyze a parsed diff to identify semantic topics, classify hunks, build a dependency DAG, and recursively decompose oversized topics. Produces a discovery.json with the topic DAG and hunk assignments. ([emilhe/split-pr](https://github.com/emilhe/split-pr/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-discovery.md))
- **pr-dispatcher**: Slot-filler watcher lane — the continuous form of `/dispatch`'s dev slots. Each tick it claims groomed, unblocked, unclaimed issues, spawns an `implementer` per issue, tracks each to an open PR, hands that PR to the watcher lanes, and refills the freed slot. Honors the label control plane (do-not-dispatch holds, agent-claimed locks, agent-model/agent-effort pins) and never steals another operator's claim. Scoped to one repo. Run with "/loop &lt;interval&gt; start". ([skyfox675/agents-skills](https://github.com/skyfox675/agents-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-dispatcher.md) · ★11 · 7 copies)
- **pr-docs**: 검증 완료된 변경 사항의 커밋, PR, 문서 초안을 준비하는 문서 작성자 ([siontext/Harness-Marketplace](https://github.com/siontext/Harness-Marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/core/agents/pr-docs.md) · ★3)
- **pr-docs-reviewer**: Reviews repository documentation, agent instructions, operational runbooks, and implementation/source-of-truth drift ([nyeinpyaesone-ui/ERP](https://github.com/nyeinpyaesone-ui/ERP/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/pr-docs-reviewer.md))
- **pr-doctor**: Repo-hygiene guardian for kudbee-music PRs. Verifies field/class names against real source before landing changes, catches CSS Modules collisions, enforces CLAUDE.md's living-state-sync + green-loop rules, and drives a branch's CI to green without leaving stray/reverted-in-message-only diffs behind. Use before pushing any PR, or to clean up a branch that's had repeated CI failures. ([KudbeeZero/kudbee-music](https://github.com/KudbeeZero/kudbee-music/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-doctor.md) · ★1)

- **pr-drafter**: Generates a PR title and body from the diff and plan context. Use at workflow step 8 alongside commit-drafter. ([krulewis/stashtrend](https://github.com/krulewis/stashtrend/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-drafter.md) · ★1)
